   - `lazy-loading.md`: **`server:defer`**: syntax and the `fallback` slot (the only one allowed), why it matters (responses are buffered, so a slow component delays the first byte unless deferred), JSON-serializable props and the generate-time error, encryption (HMAC-SHA256 nonce plus AES-256-GCM, deterministic, so the same props give the same URL), the build ID in the URL, replayable tokens (authorize in the component, never via props), `ORBIT_KEY`/`orbit create-key` and rolling deploys (instances of one build share its key), the endpoint passing through the user's middleware (locals, cookies, CSRF exemption), `ctx.URL` being the island request and reading the page URL from `Referer`, the default `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, status codes and the fallback on errors, GET vs. POST for large props, nested islands, no JS. **`client:*`**: the four strategies, `rootMargin`, whole-subtree scope (eager-reachability rule, nested directives), eager wins, the `<orbit-lazy>` wrapper (`display:contents`, child selectors), duplicated shared modules, and custom elements as the script pattern for lazy and deferred components. Inline loader and CSP.
   - `reference/`: `template-syntax.md` (full grammar table), `runtime-api.md` (the `orbit` package), `cli.md` (including that `-trimpath` doesn't trim `.orbit` paths in stack traces, §3.3, and `orbit create-key`).
   - Code blocks are tagged with their target file (```` ```orbit file=src/pages/index.orbit ````). Milestone 11 adds a test that assembles each page's tagged blocks into a temp project and builds it, so the docs can't go stale.
2. **Runtime — DONE**: `Writer` (typed writes including `Float32`, `Expr` order on exact types, pooled buffer), escaping with the zero-alloc fast path, `Attr`/`Spread` with URL filtering and name validation, the always-on depth guard, `Slots`, pooled `Ctx` with its param storage and `Param`, the segment-trie router and priority (a param match is 0 allocs, asserted with `testing.AllocsPerRun`), head injection at the marker, with unit tests.
3. **Template lexer/parser — DONE**: golden tests for attributes of every kind, nested braces in Go strings and composite literals, control blocks, void and raw-text elements, the `<` text rule, mismatched tags (error positions), and tolerant recovery. Plus a fuzz test so the parser never panics.
4. **Frontmatter and codegen — DONE**: hoisting (named `func` only; `func init` rejected), `_ = x` collection (`echo` source-map entries) and the terminating-last-statement rule, the decl/impl split (`Props` closure, the unexported-name error, the decl-export clash error, the `Render` wrapper, `_impl` package names, the var-initializer link), import partitioning by object resolution (including the implicit `orbit` import), `orbit__` identifiers with private `render` params and user-name bindings, glue through `orbit__rt`, the redeclaration check, `.orbit` import rewrite, attribute → field matching against target `Props` with the unknown-prop pre-check, the page-`Props` error, a page's generated `Params` (field names via the props PascalCase/initialism rules, the `Params` hook argument and the wrapper's `ctx.Param` calls, `type Params struct{}` without params, the page-`Params` declaration error, `params` in the redeclaration check), component calls and slots, `<Self>`, merged `Raw` constants, the `</head>` marker, the `NoLocals` path, relative line directives (inline spans with resets, own-line `//line` around hoisted declarations, field-key directives), source map, and the CLI's error-output cleanup. Then the go/types pass for typed writes (`go list -export` + `importer.ForCompiler`, decl/impl from source in parallel, `Expr` fallback). Golden tests compile the generated code in a temp module, including a self-recursive tree, an A↔B mutual recursion case and a nested `{for _, ctx := range …}` around a component call; the line-directive position test (§3.3), the typed-vs-`Expr` output test, and `go vet` clean on all generated code.

Then `orbit check`: the Go side and the TS side via `bun check` with position mapping.

Plus the LSP integration tests in §13.

## 12. Out of scope (later)
- First-class API endpoint files (for now, users write plain handlers in their own server, §5b)
- Content collections (typed sets of `.md` files with a frontmatter schema)
- Image optimization
- i18n
- HMR (dev uses full-page live reload)
- **Static-site generation (SSG), by design**: no prerendering, `StaticPaths` or static output. Orbit is SSR-only; for static sites, use Astro.
- **No framework islands, by design**: no React/Preact/Svelte/Vue/Solid components and no hydration. Importing a `.tsx`, `.jsx`, `.svelte` or `.vue` file is a compile error (§2.2), and `client:only` is rejected (§2.3). Client code is vanilla `<script>` modules in `.orbit` components, which `client:*` can load lazily, and `server:defer` covers deferred server rendering.

**Deferred past MVP** (planned, but not in the first release):
- **Order after the MVP** (user decision, after milestone 13): dogfood Orbit on a real site first, then `orbit fmt`, the typed server→client bridge, and cacheable server islands; the rest after. Added to the list after `orbit fmt` (user decision, 2026-10-26; not ordered yet): `{switch}` blocks, a custom `src/pages/500.orbit` (below), esbuild code splitting (implemented, §5e).
- **Markdown** (user decision, after milestone 13; designed after the dogfood report): `src/pages/*.md` pages with frontmatter (`title`, `layout` pointing at an `.orbit` layout that receives it), and `.md` imports into `.orbit` files (`Post "./post.md"`, rendered with `<Post />`). Rendered by goldmark **at generate time** into precomputed HTML constants: no runtime cost, the runtime stays stdlib-only, goldmark becomes a CLI dependency. Docs first, then implementation. Code blocks are highlighted at generate time with Chroma (`goldmark-highlighting`, CLI dependency; user decision at the dogfood checkpoint). **Implemented: §2.4**, user docs `docs/markdown.md`; Orbit's own Chroma renderer replaced goldmark-highlighting (user decision).
- **Dev-loop speed work** (user decision, 2026-10-26, after a `go build`/link investigation; 200 files, template edit `go build` ~205 → ~160 ms, worst case on 4 cores ~1.2 → ~0.96 s): (1) hide `islandcrypto.Sealer` from `Ctx`'s type graph (generated objects −37%); (2) dev `go build` with `-ldflags=-s -w`, `-buildvcs=false`, `-gcflags=all=-dwarf=false` (one-time std recompile per toolchain), and without `GOGC`/`GOMEMLIMIT` in its environment; (3) after the Markdown merge, move the blank impl imports from `orbit_gen/app` into a leaf `orbit_gen/link` package, so a template edit doesn't recompile `app`; (4) codegen speed-ups: per-file front in `orbit dev`, parallel front and pass 2, `go list` overlapped with parsing. Rejected: `-linkshared` (10× slower), external linking, plugins, `-trimpath`, `-c`/`-p` tuning. Not approved: skipping no-op relinks.
  - **(3) and (4) implemented** (200 files, Ryzen 7 9700X, `bench/devloop` and `orbit generate`): `orbit_gen/link` (a body edit compiles impl + `link`, 8–12 ms, instead of impl + `app`, 21–38 ms; that edit's `go build` 217 → 204 ms on 16 threads, 216 → 193 ms on 4 cores; not sharded, §3.1). `project.Reload`, the per-file front: front 9–10 → 0.6 ms (14 → 0.6 ms on 4 cores), for `orbit dev` once `internal/dev` calls it. `NormalizeName` built a `strings.Replacer` per call (21% of `orbit generate`'s CPU): front and pass 2 roughly halved. Front and pass 2 were already parallel; `Load`'s reads and `WriteGenerated` now are too (write 6.3 → 2.3 ms on a fresh `orbit_gen/`). `go list -export` overlapped (`Checker.Preload`, §3.3): `orbit generate` 100 → 69 ms (16 threads), 112 → 88 ms (4 cores); a `.go` edit's check stage 41 → 13 ms (48 → 25 ms on 4 cores).
- **Dogfood follow-ups — implemented** (user decisions, docs site): scoped CSS switches from `:where([data-o-…])` to attribute selectors (`[data-o-…]`, +1 specificity, Astro's default `scopedStyleStrategy: "attribute"`), so a component's scoped rules beat global element rules whatever the bundle order; `orbit.json` `"watch"` globs (relative to the project) make `orbit dev` restart the app and reload on changes outside `src/`.
- **`orbit fmt`** (also LSP `textDocument/formatting`): gofmt-style flags `-l -w -d`. **Implemented: §6.1.** The plan so far, which §6.1 expands:
  - **Phase 1** (Go parts only, which can never change rendered output): format frontmatter chunks with `go/format`. Declarations are formatted as file-level code; body statements are wrapped in a dummy func and dedented. Comments are kept, and imports are sorted.
  - **Then** template expressions and control headers, via Go's expression printer.
  - **Later**: safe HTML reindent, between block-level elements only, never touching inline text, `<pre>` or `<textarea>`.
  - **Script/style**: piped through `bun fmt --stdin-filepath=<n>.ts|.css` (Bun ≥ 1.4.4's Prettier-compatible formatter; verified on TS and CSS). Skipped with a notice without Bun. The **HTML** template can't be passed to `bun fmt` directly: it rewrites `title={x}` to `title="{x}"` and merges control blocks onto one line. HTML formatting would need Orbit syntax masked first; evaluate it then.
  - **Tests**: idempotence (fmt∘fmt = fmt), AST equivalence, and rendered-output equality.
- **Typed server→client bridge**: implemented (§5c).
- **TypeScript editor support inside `.orbit` `<script>`**: specified in §9b (proposed, (Q) points open).
- **Tailwind CSS** (user decision, 2026-10-26, from the `nyth` port): implemented (§5d): built-in support through Tailwind's CLI as an external, optional tool, turned on by `@import "tailwindcss"` (or Tailwind's at-rules) in a stylesheet; `internal/tailwind`, user docs `docs/tailwind.md`.
- `{switch}` template blocks: implemented (§2.3): compiler, `orbit fmt`, the LSP's Go regions, the VS Code grammar and the docs' Chroma lexer; user docs `docs/reference/template-syntax.md#switch`.
- `class:list`: implemented (§2.3): runtime (`classlist.go`), compiler, typed pass, `orbit fmt`, the LSP, the VS Code grammar; user docs `docs/reference/template-syntax.md#classlist`.
- **Concurrent rendering of slow components** (user decision, 2026-10-26, from the `nyth` port: Astro 7 overlaps awaiting sibling components, Orbit renders in order): an opt-in call-site directive (working name `server:concurrent`) runs a slow component in its own goroutine and buffer, copied back in source order at the end of the render; automatic concurrency rejected (it charges every component). Specified in §3.6 (proposed, (Q) points open).
- **Cacheable server islands** (the MVP already makes island URLs deterministic and puts the build ID in the path, §3.4, so no URL format change is needed):
  - an `ETag` on island responses (a hash of the buffered body), answering `If-None-Match` with a 304;
  - docs on opting into public caching by setting `Cache-Control` from the component (`ctx.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=60, stale-while-revalidate=600")`), with a clear warning that an island cached as `public` must not depend on cookies, locals or the user (docs only, no checks);
  - a benchmark of repeat-view TTFB with a cached island.
  HTTP caching only: no in-memory island cache in Orbit (SSR caching stays deferred, below). Tests: ETag present and stable for the same body, `If-None-Match` gives a 304 with no body.
- Slots other than `fallback` on `server:defer` components (Astro encrypts and forwards them)
- Loading islands inside fragments inserted after page load (`RenderComponent`/htmx partials) on pages without the islands loader (§5b)
- CSP hashes for the inline loader (§5)
- `site.RenderPage` (full page with assets from a custom handler)
- A custom `src/pages/500.orbit` error page (the MVP serves a minimal built-in 500 page and logs the error, §4)
- Astro's other route shapes: segments that mix text and a parameter (`post-[id]`) and rest params before other segments (`[...path]/edit`); a build error in the MVP (§4)
- Tests of embedding in chi/Gin
- The `base` config option
- **`trailingSlash` option** (user decision, 2026-10-26, prompted by porting `nyth`, an Astro site with `trailingSlash: "always"`): `orbit.json` `"trailingSlash": "never"` (default, today's behavior, §4) or `"always"` (`/x` gets a 308 to `/x/`, query kept; `/` unchanged; files and paths with a file extension never redirected); no `"ignore"` mode (two URLs per page). Reaches the router, generated URLs (`.md` `Post.URL`, rewritten `.md` links), dev and docs. Spec: §4e. **Implemented** (308 for every method, user decision; `orbit.WithTrailingSlash` passed by `app.New()` before the caller's options).
- **From the `nyth` assessment** (user decisions 2026-10-26; specs first, (Q) points open): pass-through attributes (§2.5, an `orbit.RestAttrs` field collects a call site's undeclared attributes, spread with `{...props.Attrs}`), an error → response hook (§4c, `orbit.WithErrorHandler`, before the built-in 500), and rendering a slot to a string (§4d, `orbit.RenderHTML(ctx, slots.Default)`, for fragment caches).
- **Conditional attribute values** (nice-to-have, from the `nyth` assessment): `orbit.If(cond, v)` (nil omits the attribute; lowered to an `if` by the typed pass) and `orbit.Cond(c, a, b)`, plus Astro's rule for booleans on non-boolean attributes (`aria-pressed={false}` → `"false"`). Specified in §2.6 (proposed, (Q) points open).
- **Trusted CSS values** (nice-to-have, from the `nyth` assessment): `orbit.CSS`, a marker type that silences the `style={…}` warning, which moves to the typed pass and exempts constants. Specified in §2.7 (proposed, (Q) points open).
- **The matched route for middleware** (nice-to-have, from the `nyth` assessment: its page log records the route pattern and params): `r, route := orbit.EnsureRouteInfo(r)` before `next`, filled by Orbit (pattern, file, params). Specified in §4g (proposed, (Q) points open).
- **Loading data concurrently** (user direction 2026-10-26, from the `nyth` assessment: "load all the data at the page level"): page-level loading becomes the documented main pattern, with a runtime helper `orbit.All(ctx, loads...)` (first error in time, a cancellable context per call, the failing load's position, panics re-panicked on the caller) and a documented per-request memoizing loader in `locals`; `server:concurrent` (§3.6) stays the porting escape hatch. Specified and implemented in §4f.
- SSR response caching
- The docs dogfood site (`docs/site/`, docs built with Orbit)
- **Typed form actions** (user idea, like Astro Actions): a typed, validated handler for a form or `fetch` call, declared next to the page or in `src/actions`, with an input struct decoded and validated (a Zod-like Go validator such as Zog, or struct tags, chosen then; ask before adding a dependency), errors returned to the page, and progressive enhancement without JS. Until then, users write a plain handler in `cmd/server` and render the response with `orbit.RenderComponent`.

## 13. Verification
- `go test ./...` covers:
  - runtime escaping and router tests, including `ctx.Param` (`""` for an absent name, rest values, percent-decoding) and a 0-alloc param match (`testing.AllocsPerRun`), and the param storage reset when a `Ctx` returns to the pool
  - compiler golden tests (`-update` flag to regenerate)
  - generated code compiling in a temp module
  - line-directive positions in `go build` and `go vet` output, and the CLI's cleaned-up error output (§3.3)
  - Orbit's own codegen errors: `<Card psot={p}>` reports `unknown prop "psot" on <Card> (props: post, featured)` at the attribute; a frontmatter `func init`, a decl-export clash, a template after a terminating statement, `type Props` in a page (`pages don't receive props; move this to a component or read params`), `type Params` in a page, and a top-level `params := …` in a page are reported at the `.orbit` position; two route params with colliding field names (`[post-id]/[postId].orbit`) and a bad param name (`[1st].orbit`) fail at the file
  - lazy-loading compile errors, each at its `.orbit` position (§2.2, §2.3, §3.4): importing `./Chart.tsx` (and `.jsx`, `.svelte`, `.vue`) gives `framework components aren't supported; use a .orbit component with a <script>`; `./util.ts` gives the relative-path error; `server:defer` with a non-`fallback` child, with a value, or on an HTML element; `client:*` on an HTML element, with an `{expr}` value, twice on one tag, or `client:media` without a value; `client:only`; `server:defer` plus `client:*` on one tag; unknown `server:x`/`client:x`; a deferred component whose `Props` has a func, chan, `unsafe.Pointer`, complex, interface (`any`, `error`) or bad-map-key field (error names the field path), while `time.Time` and `json:"-"` fields pass
  - `internal/islandcrypto`: round-trip; tampering with the nonce, the ciphertext or the associated data is rejected; a different build ID or component ID fails; the same props give the same token (determinism); a different key fails; a fuzz test for the decoder (`FuzzOpen`: never panics, accepts only tokens it sealed)
  - typed writes and the `Expr` fallback producing identical output (including `float32` and named types)
  - typed route params: golden output of `Params` for `[slug]`, `[...path]`, `[post-id]` (`PostID`), `[id]` (`ID`) and a param-less page (`struct{}`); `params.Slg` fails `go build` at its `.orbit` line:col
  - parser fuzz test (`go test -fuzz=FuzzParse -fuzztime=30s ./internal/compiler`)
  - an integration test that builds `examples/blog` into a temp dir and asserts on the HTML
- `cd examples/blog && go run ../../cmd/orbit build`:
  - `dist/server` and `dist/client/_orbit/` exist, `public/` files are in `dist/client/`, and no page is rendered to an `.html` file
  - a rendered page's `<head>` links the hashed `/_orbit/*.css` and `*.js`, after the doctype
  - the CSS contains `[data-o-…]` scoped selectors
  - elements carry the scope attribute
- `dist/server &` then `curl localhost:4321/search?q=go`: the SSR page shows `go`, with `Content-Length` and `text/html; charset=utf-8`. `curl /blog/hello` renders the post, `curl /blog/nope` returns 404 (`ErrNotFound`), `curl -i /blog/hello/` returns 308 to `/blog/hello`, `curl -i '/search/?q=go'` returns 308 to `/search?q=go`, `curl -i /` returns 200 (no redirect), a `public/` or `/_orbit/*` path with a trailing slash is not redirected, `/_orbit/*` responses carry the immutable cache header, and `curl /nope` returns 404 rendered from `404.orbit`. With `"trailingSlash": "always"` (`internal/build` `TestBuildTrailingSlashAlways`): `/blog/hello` and `/search?q=go` return 308 to `/blog/hello/` and `/search/?q=go` (GET, HEAD and POST, empty body), `/blog/hello/` and `/` return 200, an extension page renders at `/rss.xml`, and a `.md` page's link to another is `/notes/b/#setup`.
- Recursion: `/blog/hello`'s deferred `Comments` island (fetched from its placeholder's URL) renders a nested `CommentThread` three levels deep. A unit test checks that infinite recursion produces a depth error naming the component stack, with the guard on in prod mode.
- `set:json`: a value containing `</script><script>alert(1)</script>` and U+2028 renders as inert, escaped JSON that `JSON.parse` round-trips. `set:json` on a non-JSON `<script>` is a compile error.
- Security: `href={"javascript:alert(1)"}` renders `about:invalid#orbit`, and an `orbit.URL` value passes; `set:html={s}` with a `string` variable fails `orbit build` at the `.orbit` line; a spread with an invalid attribute name is a render error.
- Custom routes:
  - `curl /api/posts` returns JSON.
  - `curl -X POST /blog/hello/like` returns the `LikeButton` HTML fragment.
  - Unit tests with `httptest` cover both compositions. In mux-first mode, a custom route wins over a page at the same path. In Orbit-first mode, the page wins (for every method), a POST to an `/_orbit/*` or `public/` path reaches `next`, unknown paths reach `next` untouched (no bytes written, no headers set), and `site.NotFound()` as the mux catch-all renders `404.orbit`. Wrapping middleware sees page requests.
- Middleware and locals: `curl -b theme=dark /account` shows `dark` and a request ID, both set by the net/http middleware via `EnsureLocals`. Unit tests: a second `EnsureLocals` returns the same pointer and the same `*http.Request`; `LocalsFromRequest` returns nil when nothing is attached. A typo such as `locals.Them` fails `orbit build` with the `.orbit` line, and the LSP completes `locals.` with `RequestID` and `Theme`. A header that an outer net/http middleware adds appears on both `dist/server` and dev responses. A unit test checks that a wrapping `ResponseWriter` sees the full page HTML with assets injected. A project without `src/locals` builds with `orbit.NoLocals`; a `src/locals` that imports `orbit_gen/app` is reported clearly.
- Lazy loading (`server:defer`, `client:*`):
  - **Fallback without JS**: `curl /blog/hello` returns the post with `<orbit-island src="/_orbit/island/<buildID>/Comments-…" props="…">` holding `Loading comments…`, the inline loader and the `display:contents` style in `<head>`, and is not delayed by `Comments`' simulated slow query (its TTFB is measured below that delay). A page with no islands and no lazy components has no loader.
  - **Endpoint**: a GET of the placeholder's URL returns the comments fragment with `Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8`, `Content-Length`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` and no injected assets; a component that sets `Cache-Control` through `ctx.Header()` keeps its own. A POST with the token as the body returns the same fragment. A flipped byte in the token, a token moved to another component's or another build ID's path, or a token sealed with another key gets a 400; an unknown component ID a 404; a PUT a 405 with `Allow: GET, HEAD, POST`; a POST body over 1 MiB a 413. `ErrNotFound` in an island gives an empty 404, a render error a 500.
  - **Deterministic URLs**: the same props give the same island URL across two renders and across two instances of one build; a different build ID (a second `orbit build`) gives a different URL, which still opens when both builds share `ORBIT_KEY`.
  - **Key**: two processes with the same `ORBIT_KEY` open each other's tokens; without it, two separate builds don't; an invalid `ORBIT_KEY` (bad base64, wrong length) fails at startup with a clear message; `WithKey` overrides the env; `orbit create-key` prints 32 bytes of base64.
  - **Middleware**: a header added by an outer net/http middleware appears on island responses, the island sees locals set by `EnsureLocals` and the request's cookies, `ctx.URL` is the island URL, and `ctx.Request.Referer()` is the page URL.
  - **Large props**: a deferred component given more than 8000 characters of token is loaded with a POST.
  - **Eager wins**: a page that renders `<Counter/>` and `<Counter client:visible/>` has Counter's script in its page bundle and no `lazy` asset for it; a page with only `<Counter client:visible/>` has a lazy entry for it that isn't in the page bundle; the wrapper is `<orbit-lazy component="Counter-…" client="visible">`, and `client:load` emits no wrapper.
  - **Loader** (`bun test` + happy-dom, with IntersectionObserver, requestIdleCallback, matchMedia and fetch mocked; milestone 8): islands are fetched in parallel and replace their placeholders; a nested deferred island inside a fetched fragment loads; a 500 keeps the fallback and logs to the console in dev only; a token over 8000 characters is POSTed; `client:visible`, `client:idle` and `client:media` each import their entry once, at the right moment, also for lazy components inside a fetched island; with JS disabled, fallbacks stay.
- `orbit dev`:
  - Edit `Card.orbit`: the browser reloads with the change. A Go test subscribes to `/__orbit/events` and asserts a `reload` event arrives after a file write.
  - A CSS-only edit triggers no Go rebuild, and the page links the new hashed URL (via `manifest.json`). Adding a component's first `<style>` triggers a Go rebuild, and the scope attributes appear.
  - The reload script is injected under a user gzip middleware. A custom `/robots.txt` handler behaves the same in dev as in prod.
  - Introduce a type error (`{props.Psot}`): the overlay shows `src/components/Card.orbit:L:C`. Fix it: the page reloads automatically.
- Fresh clone: a copy of `examples/blog` without `orbit_gen/` passes `go tool orbit generate && go vet ./... && go build ./...`. `orbit new` produces a project whose `go.mod` has a `toolchain` line, where `go build ./...` succeeds and `go tool orbit build` produces `dist/server` with no edits, and a global `orbit` re-execs `go tool orbit` inside it.
- Docs: the docs-examples test builds every tagged code block in `docs/`.
- TypeScript: the example's `<script>` uses a typed function and imports `src/scripts/util.ts`. The built bundle contains no type annotations. A `<script type="application/ld+json">` is left untouched. With Bun installed, `orbit check` reports a deliberate TS error (`const n: number = "x"`) at the correct `.orbit` line:col; without Bun it prints the skip notice and still runs the Go half.
- Single binary: `orbit build --embed`, then copy only `dist/server` to an empty temp dir and run it there. Pages, `/_orbit/*` assets and `public/` files are all served.
- Performance: `bench/go` runs Orbit, templ and `html/template` on the same page. `docs/performance.md` has req/s, p50/p99 and RSS for the head-to-head scenarios, with Orbit at `GOMAXPROCS=1` and at all cores, plus page TTFB for the deferred 10 ms-fetch page next to the non-deferred one (no target number, no CI gates).
- Toolchain: `go version` in the repo and in `examples/blog` reports go1.27.3.
- LSP integration test (it spawns `orbit lsp` over stdio, with gopls from PATH; skipped with a notice if absent):
  - completion after `{props.` lists `Post` and `Featured`
  - `<Card ` offers `post` and `featured`
  - hovering a frontmatter variable shows its type
  - a misspelled prop produces a diagnostic on the attribute
  - definition on `<Card` jumps to `Card.orbit`
  - in `src/pages/blog/[slug].orbit`, completion after `params.` lists `Slug`, and definition on `params.Slug` goes to the page file
  - adding a field to `Card`'s `Props` in an unsaved buffer makes the new attribute valid in a parent, and nothing is written to `orbit_gen/`
  - renaming a frontmatter variable updates its template uses (the `echo` edit is dropped); renaming `Props.Post` to `Article` rewrites `post=` to `article=` at every call site; a rename that would touch unmapped code is rejected with a message; `prepareRename` on a position that maps only to glue is refused
  - hover never shows `line …` directive text
  - on a component tag, completion offers `server:defer` and the four `client:*` directives; on an HTML element it doesn't; `slot="` inside a `server:defer` component offers only `fallback`
  - in overlay-only mode (no `orbit_gen/` on disk) with at least 50 components, references and rename are complete once the readiness barrier finishes
  - positions after non-ASCII text (`"Héllo ✓"`) round-trip correctly in UTF-16
  - a gopls built with an older Go than the project's `go` directive (a stub `serverInfo`) disables Go features and shows the install command, while native completion still works
- TypeScript integration test (§9b; `orbit lsp` on a copy of `examples/blog` whose `tsconfig.json` lacks `rootDirs`, with no `orbit_gen/`, and TypeScript 7 from `ORBIT_TEST_TYPESCRIPT` or the lookup without `bun x`; skipped with a notice if absent): in `LikeButton.orbit`'s script, completion in `data(this, "` offers `like`; hover on `like.likes` shows `number`; a typo gives a `ts` diagnostic at the `.orbit` line:col; definition goes into `util.ts`, from `data` onto the `data-like` attribute and from `likes` onto the Go field; references of `plural` include a closed `.orbit` file; renaming a script local works and renaming `likes` is refused; an unsaved `json` tag change in the frontmatter turns `like.likes` red; an `is:inline` script gets JavaScript completion, hover and diagnostics; nothing is written. Stub-child tests cover routing, the child's requests (never an error), the refresh, crash and one restart, and the one-time notice without an engine.
- Manual check: open `examples/blog` in VS Code with the extension in development mode (F5) and confirm highlighting, completion and diagnostics.
- `orbittest`: a test in `examples/blog/internal/views_test` renders `Card` with props and a default slot, and asserts on the HTML. A `Snapshot` round-trips with `-update`. `orbittest.Get(t, site.Handler(), "/blog/hello")` returns 200 with the page. `orbittest.Render` of the `blog/[slug]` page with `WithParam("slug", "hello")` renders that post; `WithParam` passed to `Get` fails the test. A test that omits the `orbit_gen/app` import fails with the "not linked" hint.
- Decisions logged in `docs/DECISIONS.md`: `orbit.ListenAndServe` returns nil after SIGINT (the scaffold's `if err := …; err != nil { log.Fatal(err) }` exits 0) and honors the 5 s grace period; `post-[id].orbit` and `[...path]/edit.orbit` fail the build with the whole-segment `[param]` hint; a duplicate route names both files; a page error in prod gives the built-in 500 page and logs the error with its `.orbit` position; `orbittest.Render` of a page with an island returns the same placeholder on two runs; `orbit new x --module example.com/x` writes that module path (and the directory name without the flag); `orbit dev` isn't reachable on a non-loopback address without `--host`; CSS imported from a script is in the page stylesheet; exit code 2 on an unknown flag.
- `orbit doctor`: on a healthy `orbit new` project, every check passes. With an old gopls first on PATH, it reports ✗ with the install command. With `orbit_gen/` deleted, it reports ⚠ suggesting `orbit generate`. `--json` output parses.
- Dockerfile: `docker build` of a fresh, unmodified `orbit new` project succeeds. `docker run -p 8080:8080` serves the index page and its `/_orbit/*` CSS, and `/nope` returns the 404 page. `docker run -e PORT=9090 -p 9090:9090` listens on 9090 (the platform's `PORT` wins). The image is under 30 MB.
